Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The ceramics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 355 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 756 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to ceramics theory, we decompose the problem into 355 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 756. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the ceramics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 224 and the convergence rate is O(308/n^2).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the ceramics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^224 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 308-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
